### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para poner tu rama al día con la principal y mantener el historial lineal, siempre que la rama sea solo tuya y no la hayas compartido. Usa merge cuando la rama ya la usan otros o quieres conservar tal cual cómo ocurrieron los cambios.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 面向连接、可靠有序地传数据，UDP 不建连接、不保证送达和顺序，只管尽快发出去。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase when the branch is yours alone and not pushed yet, so you can tidy commits or catch up with main without a merge commit.
Merge when others already have the branch or you want the history to show exactly when the work joined.
```

  [opened a task] title: 修复不稳定的 TTL 测试 | place: {"kind":"repo","device":"local","repoPath":"~/dev/foo"} | runs on computer: the home (Ducter's home, called "local" in a place) | Ducter gave it to: claude-code (opus) | brief: 用户原话："修一下 ~/dev/foo 里那个不稳定的 TTL 测试，修好告诉我" ⏎  ⏎ 决定事项： ⏎ - 目标是 ~/dev/foo 里那个时好时坏（flaky）的 TTL 相关测试。 ⏎ - 先定位这个测试（搜索 TTL / expire / ttl 相关的测试文件），确认它为什么不稳定。常见原因是用真实时钟或 sleep 等待过期，在机器忙时会抢跑或超时。若确实如此，改用假时钟（fake timers）或注入可控的时间源，让测试确定性地通过。 ⏎ - 修改只限于让测试稳定，不改被测的 TTL 行为本身，除非发现是真实 bug（那种情况先停下来报告，不要擅自改业务逻辑）。 ⏎  ⏎ 完成标准（都要有证据写进报告）： ⏎ - 说明测试不稳定的原因。 ⏎ - 给出改动的文件和思路。 ⏎ - 连续多次运行该测试（例如 20 次）全部通过，并附上命令和结果摘要。 ⏎ - 跑一遍相关测试套件，确认没有新失败。 ⏎  ⏎ 范围外： ⏎ - 不要合并到主分支，不要推送，不要开 PR。改动只留在任务自己的分支上，由用户决定之后怎么处理。 ⏎ - 不要改无关的测试或代码。 ⏎  ⏎ 地点：~/dev/foo（文件夹）。 ⏎  ⏎ The things asked (the user sees each under their message, each ticking off on its own): ⏎ 1. 修好那个不稳定的 TTL 测试 ⏎ 2. 修好后告诉我 ⏎ In report_to_main, give each one's state in `asks`, in this order, with a short note of what came of it (the answer, "added", "PR opened"). A progress report with `asks` ticks off the ones already done while you go on.
--- under the user's message: 1. 修好那个不稳定的 TTL 测试 (working); 2. 修好后告诉我 (working)
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 用文件路径直接指定就行：

```bash
bun test test/user.test.ts
```

也可以只写文件名的一部分，Bun 会匹配路径里包含这段文字的测试文件：

```bash
bun test user
```

如果想只跑某个测试用例，用 `-t`（或 `--test-name-pattern`）按名字过滤：

```bash
bun test test/user.test.ts -t "登录"
```
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is a file where a database records every change before applying it to the main data. If the system crashes mid-write, it replays that log on restart to recover the changes that were committed and roll back the ones that weren't.
```

--- step 1: user answers the open decision: go
  [partner message] Board's ready, filled with the Night 2 and Night 3 numbers. It'll update each night.
  [an app card under the message: made "Nightly hawdb writes", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1822 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <section class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Latest writes/s</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="latest">—</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="latest-sub">Nothing reported yet</div>
  </section>
  <h2 class="k-h">Nights</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="nights"></div>
  </div>
  <div class="k-empty" id="empty" hidden>The nightly run will fill this in each round.</div>
</main>
<script>
function fmt(n){ return n >= 1000 ? Math.round(n/1000) + 'k' : String(n); }
function render(nights){
  var list = document.getElementById('nights');
  var empty = document.getElementById('empty');
  list.innerHTML = '';
  if (!nights || !nights.length) {
    document.getElementById('latest').textContent = '—';
    document.getElementById('latest-sub').textContent = 'Nothing reported yet';
    empty.hidden = false;
    return;
  }
  empty.hidden = true;
  var last = nights[nights.length - 1];
  document.getElementById('latest').textContent = fmt(last.writesPerSec) + '/s';
  document.getElementById('latest-sub').textContent = 'p99 ' + last.p99Ms + ' ms · ' + last.stalls + ' compaction stalls';
  nights.slice().reverse().forEach(function(n){
    var row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'k-row';
    row.innerHTML = '<div class="k-row-main">' + n.label + '</div>' +
      '<div class="k-row-sub">p99 ' + n.p99Ms + ' ms · ' + n.stalls + ' stalls</div>' +
      '<div class="k-row-value">' + fmt(n.writesPerSec) + '/s</div>';
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
}
var nightsSeed = ducter.get("nightsSeed") || [];
ducter.get('nights').then(function(v){
  var nights = Array.isArray(v) && v.length ? v : nightsSeed;
  render(nights);
});
ducter.onChange(function(){
  ducter.get('nights').then(function(v){ if (Array.isArray(v)) render(v); });
});
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"nights":[{"label":"Night 2","writesPerSec":181000,"p99Ms":4,"stalls":0},{"label":"Night 3","writesPerSec":182000,"p99Ms":4.1,"stalls":0}],"nightsSeed":[{"label":"Night 2","writesPerSec":181000,"p99Ms":4,"stalls":0},{"label":"Night 3","writesPerSec":182000,"p99Ms":4.1,"stalls":0}]}

    if (!b) return;
    cur = +b.getAttribute('data-i');
    pend = -1;
    render();
  });

  $('addDay').addEventListener('click', function () {
    S.days.push({ name: '第' + (S.days.length + 1) + '天', items: [] });
    cur = S.days.length - 1;
    pend = -1;
    save(); render();
  });

  $('items').addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var r = e.target.closest('.k-row');
    if (!r) return;
    var i = +r.getAttribute('data-r');
    if (pend === i) {
      S.days[cur].items.splice(i, 1);
      pend = -1;
      save();
    } else {
      pend = i;
    }
    render();
  });

  $('save').addEventListener('click', function () {
    var name = $('nm').value.trim();
    var amount = parseFloat($('am').value);
    if (!name || !(amount >= 0)) return;
    S.days[cur].items.push({ name: name, amount: Math.round(amount * 100) / 100 });
    $('nm').value = '';
    $('am').value = '';
    pend = -1;
    save(); render();
  });

  var got = ducter.get('trip');
  if (got && typeof got.then === 'function') got.then(load); else load(got);
  if (ducter.onChange) ducter.onChange(load);
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {}

  .k-row { display: block; }

  .k-check { width: 18px; height: 18px; margin-top: 3px; accent-color: var(--d-accent, #4a6cf7); }
  .k-pick > span { flex: 1; }
</style>
<script>
(function () {
  var hasD = typeof ducter !== 'undefined';
  var DEFAULT = ducter.get("default") || [];

  function items() {
    var saved = hasD ? ducter.get('prs') : null;
    return saved && saved.length ? saved : DEFAULT;
  }
  function landed() {
    return (hasD && ducter.get('landed')) || {};
  }

  function row(item, done) {
    var li = document.createElement('li');
    li.className = 'k-row' + (done ? ' done' : '');
    li.setAttribute('data-id', item.id);
    var label =  … nt.createElement('span');
    main.className = 'k-row-main';
    main.textContent = item.main;
    var sub = document.createElement('span');
    sub.className = 'k-row-sub';
    sub.textContent = item.sub;
    span.appendChild(main);
    span.appendChild(sub);
    label.appendChild(cb);
    label.appendChild(span);
    li.appendChild(label);
    cb.addEventListener('change', function () {
      var s = landed();
      s[item.id] = cb.checked;
      if (hasD) ducter.set('landed', s);
      render();
    });
    return li;
  }

  function render() {
    var list = items();
    var s = landed();
    var waiting = document.getElementById('waiting');
    var draft = document.getElementById('draft');
    waiting.innerHTML = '';
    draft.innerHTML = '';
    var open = 0;
    list.forEach(function (it) {
      var done = !!s[it.id];
      if (!done) open++;
      (it.group === 'draft' ? draft : waiting).appendChild(row(it, done));
    });
    document.getElementById('count').textContent = open;
    if (hasD && ducter.summary) ducter.summary(open + ' open, ' + (list.length - open) + ' landed');
  }

  if (hasD && ducter.onChange) ducter.onChange(render);
  render();
})();
</script>
</main>; its data now (what it opens with): {"default":[{"id":"ducter-412","group":"waiting","main":"wey-gu/ducter#412 · compaction cut","sub":"Waiting on Weizhen's review since Monday"},{"id":"mem-10287","group":"waiting","main":"nowledge-co/mem#10287 · retry on 502","sub":"CI is failing"},{"id":"hawdb-1592","group":"waiting","main":"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 · flaky compaction test","sub":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday"},{"id":"ducter-418","group":"draft","main":"wey-gu/ducter#418 · apps bar","sub":"Still a draft"}],"prs":[{"id":"ducter-412","group":"waiting","main":"wey-gu/ducter#412 · compaction cut","sub":"Waiting on Weizhen's review since Monday"},{"id":"mem-10287","group":"waiting","main":"nowledge-co/mem#10287 · retry on 502","sub":"CI is failing"},{"id":"hawdb-1592","group":"waiting","main":"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 · flaky compaction test","sub":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday"},{"id":"ducter-418","group":"draft","main":"wey-gu/ducter#418 · apps bar","sub":"Still a draft"}]}

(function () {
  var cat = '交通';
  var $ = function (id) { return document.getElementById(id); };
  function esc(s) {
    return String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) {
      return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c];
    });
  }
  function items() { return ducter.get('items … 。</div>';
      return;
    }
    $('list').innerHTML = xs.map(function (x) {
      return '<div class="k-row">' +
        '<div class="k-row-main">' + esc(x.trip || '未命名') + '</div>' +
        '<div class="k-row-sub">' + esc(x.cat || '') + (x.day ? ' · ' + esc(x.day) : '') + '</div>' +
        '<div class="k-row-value">' + money(x.amt) + '</div>' +
        '</div>';
    }).join('');
  }

  Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('#cats .k-chip'), function (b) {
    b.onclick = function () {
      cat = b.getAttribute('data-c');
      Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('#cats .k-chip'), function (o) {
        o.setAttribute('aria-pressed', o === b ? 'true' : 'false');
      });
    };
  });

  $('save').onclick = function () {
    var amt = parseFloat($('amt').value);
    if (!amt) return;
    var xs = items();
    xs.push({
      trip: $('trip').value.trim() || '出差',
      cat: cat,
      amt: amt,
      day: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)
    });
    ducter.set('items', xs);
    $('trip').value = '';
    $('amt').value = '';
    $('add') && ($('add').open = false);
    render();
  };

  ducter.onChange(render);
  render();
})();

### direct-stable-fact-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: Bun 是用什么语言写的？
  [partner message] Bun 主要用 **Zig** 写的，它是 Bun 自己的核心运行时代码。它底下的 JavaScript 引擎用的是 Apple 的 **JavaScriptCore**，这部分是 C++。Bun 内置的一些模块则用 **TypeScript** 写。
```

### picture-question-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 图里这是什么？适合放在空状态里吗？ (with a picture: dev_laptop_awake.webp)
  [partner message] 这是一个奶油色的 3D 风格笔记本电脑插画：屏幕空白，深蓝色边框，键盘和触控板都有细节。

适合放在空状态里。屏幕是空的，正好表示"还没有内容"，画风温和柔软，不会让人觉得出错了。配上一句说明和一个操作按钮就够用了，比如"还没有任何笔记，新建一个开始吧"。

要注意两点：它本身偏浅，放在深色背景上对比会弱一些，深色模式下最好给它留一块浅色底，或者换一个深色版本。另外图片只有 320×246，显示时宜控制在 120–160 像素宽，放大会模糊。
```

- 到期前一刻（少 1 毫秒左右）：值仍然有效。
- 保持使用假时钟，不要引入真实计时。
